single_squad_dev_3779
What can happen if two over-lapping Grand Lodges are in Amity and decide to share jurisdiction?
A: ["Prince Hall Freemasonry", "Grand Lodge", "Grand Lodge", "Grand Lodge of Cyprus", "Grand Lodge of Cyprus", "Grand Lodge of Canada in the Province of Ontario"]
A: Exclusive Jurisdiction can be waived when the two over-lapping Grand Lodges are themselves in Amity and agree to share jurisdiction (for example, since the Grand Lodge of Connecticut is in Amity with the Prince Hall Grand Lodge of Connecticut, the principle of Exclusive Jurisdiction
A: Exit? No.
A: ["Prince Hall Freemasonry", "Grand Lodge", "Grand Lodge", "Grand Lodge of Cyprus", "Grand Lodge of Cyprus", "Grand Lodge of Canada in the Province of Ontario", "Freemasonry", "Grand Lodge of Connecticut"]
A: Exclusive Jurisdiction can be waived when the two over-lapping Grand Lodges are themselves in Amity and agree to share jurisdiction (for example, since the Grand Lodge of Connecticut is in Amity with the Prince Hall Grand Lodge of Connecticut, the principle of Exclusive Jurisdiction So the answer is: Exclusive Jurisdiction can be waived when the two over-lapping Grand Lodges are themselves in Amity and agree to share jurisdiction (for example, since the Grand Lodge of Connecticut is in Amity with the Prince Hall Grand Lodge of Connecticut, the principle of Exclusive Jurisdiction does not apply, and other Grand Lodges may recognise both).

When did the First Macedonian War begin?
A: ["First Macedonian War", "First Macedonian War", "Macedonian Wars", "Macedonian Wars", "Macedonian Wars", "Macedonian Wars"]
A: The First Macedonian War (214–205 BC) was fought by Rome, allied (after 211 BC) with the Aetolian League and Attalus I of Pergamon, against Philip V of Macedon, contemporaneously with the Second Punic War (218–201 BC) against Carthage.

A: The 2000-watt society is an environmental vision, first introduced in 1998 by the Swiss Federal Institute of Technology in Zürich (ETH Zurich), which pictures the average First World citizen reducing their overall average primary energy usage to no more than 2,000 watts (i.e. 2 kilowatt-hours per hour or 48 kWh per day) by the year 2050, without lowering their standard of living.
